- **state-compactor**: Zero-loss state protocol for long agent sessions: continuous writes to.agent_state.json and STATE.md while the context is still sharp, so a fresh window can be rehydrated with 100% fact parity, plus cgate for context-gated reading of large files through a local LLM. Use when a long session is getting confused or drifting, to keep the facts straight across a compaction, to hand state to a subagent, or to slice a huge file instead of reading it whole.
